// название, назначение и кнопка ⚙ (44 px на экране) — в середине помещения; не помещаются — ⚙ в правом верхнем углу
function roomLabelSvg(r,k){
  // область подписи: без полосы внутренних цепочек размеров у стен, к которым вплотную соседнее помещение
  const ins={l:0,r:0,t:0,b:0}, cin=layerOn('dims')?Math.max(85,60+20/k):0;
  if(S.rooms.length>1) for(const sd of blockedSides(r)) if(r.openings.some(o=>o.side===sd)) ins[sd]=cin;
  const ir=hasPoly(r)?innerRect(r):null, X0=ir?ir.x*100:ins.l, Y0=ir?ir.y*100:ins.t, W=Math.max(0,ir?ir.w*100:r.w*100-ins.l-ins.r), D=Math.max(0,ir?ir.h*100:r.d*100-ins.t-ins.b), q=v=>+v.toFixed(2), name=r.name||'Помещение', G=44/k, m=10/k;
  const rt=r.roles.map(x=>(ROLES.find(z=>z[0]===x)||[])[1]).filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  const fs=Math.max(13/k, Math.min(26/k, (W-G-3*m)/(name.length*0.6+0.5))), nw=name.length*0.6*fs, fr=11.5/k;
  const rowW=nw+m+G, fit=rowW<=W-2*m && Math.max(fs*1.3,G)<=D-2*m, lab=layerOn('labels');
  g+=`<text x="${x+250}" y="${y+12}" font-family="IBMPlexSans" font-weight="bold" font-size="17">М 1:${k}</text></g>`;
  return g;
}
function needMarks(c){ const m=[];
  if(c.cw) m.push(['ХВ','--z-cold']); if(c.hw) m.push(['ГВ','--z-hot']); if(c.dr) m.push(['К','--p-sewer']);
  if((c.kw||0)>0) m.push([c.ph===3?'ЭЛ 380':'ЭЛ 220','--p-el']); if(c.gas) m.push(['Г1','--p-gas']); return m; }
function printPlanSvg(opts){
  const layer=(opts.layers||['equip'])[0], mono=!!opts.mono, pal=mono?PRINT_MONO:PRINT_PALETTE, pos=opts.pos;
  const keep={sel, selSet, selRoom, addMode, showZones}; selSet=new Set(); sel=null; selRoom=null; addMode=null; showZones=false; if(typeof scrK!=='undefined') scrK=null;   // печать — без экранных размеров подписей
  try{
    const W=S.room.w*100, D=S.room.d*100, T=10, ex=planExtents(S.room, layer), k=opts.scale||50;
    PT_SH=(layer==='points'||layer==='need')?ptShift(ptMarkSides(S.points),null):{t:0,r:0,b:0,l:0};
    PRINT_EL=(layer==='points'||layer==='need');   // зона перед щитом и круг «вода не ближе» — на листах подключений
    const oT=88+nicheOutEdge(S.room,'t',S.openings), oL=88+nicheOutEdge(S.room,'l',S.openings);   // на печати цепочка за метками всё ещё ближе общего размера
    const ink='var(--ink)', ink2='var(--ink-2)';
    let g='';
    for(let x=0;x<=W+0.1;x+=50) g+=`<line x1="${x}" y1="0" x2="${x}" y2="${D}" stroke="var(${Math.round(x)%100?'--grid':'--grid-2'})" stroke-width="1"/>`;
    for(let y=0;y<=D+0.1;y+=50) g+=`<line x1="0" y1="${y}" x2="${W}" y2="${y}" stroke="var(${Math.round(y)%100?'--grid':'--grid-2'})" stroke-width="1"/>`;
    let gridClip='';
    if(hasPoly(S.room)){ const id=`rcp-${S.room.id||'r'}`, points=contourPts(S.room,0).map(p=>`${p.x},${p.y}`).join(' '); gridClip=`<clipPath id="${id}"><polygon points="${points}"/></clipPath>`; g=`<g clip-path="url(#${id})">${g}</g>`; }
    g+=nicheGridSvg(S.room);
    const dims=`<g fill="${ink2}" font-family="var(--f-mono)" font-size="19">
      <line x1="0" y1="${-oT}" x2="${W}" y2="${-oT}" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="1.2"/><line x1="0" y1="${-oT-8}" x2="0" y2="${-oT+8}" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="1.2"/><line x1="${W}" y1="${-oT-8}" x2="${W}" y2="${-oT+8}" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="1.2"/>
      <text x="${W/2}" y="${-oT-10}" text-anchor="middle">${fmt0(S.room.w*1000)}</text>
      <line x1="${-oL}" y1="0" x2="${-oL}" y2="${D}" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="1.2"/><line x1="${-oL-8}" y1="0" x2="${-oL+8}" y2="0" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="1.2"/><line x1="${-oL-8}" y1="${D}" x2="${-oL+8}" y2="${D}" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="1.2"/>
      <text x="${-oL-10}" y="${D/2}" text-anchor="middle" transform="rotate(-90 ${-oL-10} ${D/2})">${fmt0(S.room.d*1000)}</text></g>`;
      body=walls+ops+eq+swr.lines+ptsAll+marks+nums+swr.labels+(typeof zeroMarkSvg==='function'?zs.map(s=>zeroMarkSvg(S.room,s)).join(''):'');
    }
    const bar=scaleBarSvg(0, D+104, ink, k);
    const svg=`<svg viewBox="${ex.x} ${ex.y} ${ex.w} ${ex.h}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${ex.w}" height="${ex.h}">${hatchDefs(mono, pal['--ink'])}
      <rect x="${ex.x}" y="${ex.y}" width="${ex.w}" height="${ex.h}" fill="#ffffff"/>${gridClip}${g}${dims}${body}${bar}</svg>`;
    return finalizePrintSvg(svg, pal);
  } finally { sel=keep.sel; selSet=keep.selSet; selRoom=keep.selRoom; addMode=keep.addMode; showZones=keep.showZones; PT_SH={t:0,r:0,b:0,l:0}; PRINT_EL=false; }
}
// общий план всех помещений
function printOverviewSvg(opts){
  const mono=!!(opts&&opts.mono), pal=mono?PRINT_MONO:PRINT_PALETTE, rs=S.rooms;
  const minX=Math.min(...rs.map(r=>r.x)), minY=Math.min(...rs.map(r=>r.y)), maxX=Math.max(...rs.map(r=>r.x+r.w)), maxY=Math.max(...rs.map(r=>r.y+r.d));
  const pad=Math.max(170,...rs.flatMap(r=>roomGeom(S,r).openings.filter(o=>o.type==='prot'&&o.niche).map(o=>(o.depth||0.3)*100+20))), k=100, X=v=>(v-minX)*k, Y=v=>(v-minY)*k, ink='var(--ink)', ink2='var(--ink-2)';
  let out='', arrows='';
  for(const r of rs){
    const points=hasPoly(r)?contourPts(r,0).map(p=>`${X(r.x+p.x/100)},${Y(r.y+p.y/100)}`).join(' '):'';
    out+=hasPoly(r)?`<polygon points="${points}" fill="#ffffff" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="8" stroke-linejoin="miter"/>`:`<rect x="${X(r.x)}" y="${Y(r.y)}" width="${r.w*k}" height="${r.d*k}" fill="#ffffff" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="8"/>`;
    for(const o of roomGeom(S,r).openings){ const sg=segOf({room:r},o), x1=X(r.x+sg.x1), y1=Y(r.y+sg.y1), x2=X(r.x+sg.x2), y2=Y(r.y+sg.y2);
      if(o.type==='prot'&&o.niche){ const t0=o.off*100, t1=(o.off+o.w)*100, dep=(o.depth||0.3)*100, a=wallOutU(r,o.side,t0,-5), b=wallOutU(r,o.side,t1,-5), bo=wallOutU(r,o.side,t1,dep+5), ao=wallOutU(r,o.side,t0,dep+5), a0=wallOutU(r,o.side,t0,0), b0=wallOutU(r,o.side,t1,0), ad=wallOutU(r,o.side,t0,dep), bd=wallOutU(r,o.side,t1,dep), cv=p=>[X(r.x+p[0]/100),Y(r.y+p[1]/100)], ca=cv(a), cb=cv(b), cbo=cv(bo), cao=cv(ao), ca0=cv(a0), cb0=cv(b0), cad=cv(ad), cbd=cv(bd);
        out+=`<polygon points="${ca[0]},${ca[1]} ${cb[0]},${cb[1]} ${cbo[0]},${cbo[1]} ${cao[0]},${cao[1]}" fill="#ffffff"/>`;
        out+=`<path d="M ${ca0[0]} ${ca0[1]} L ${cad[0]} ${cad[1]} L ${cbd[0]} ${cbd[1]} L ${cb0[0]} ${cb0[1]}" fill="none" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="8" stroke-linejoin="miter"/>`; continue; }
      if(o.type==='prot'){ const pr=protRect({room:r},o); out+=`<rect x="${X(r.x+pr.x)}" y="${Y(r.y+pr.y)}" width="${pr.w*k}" height="${pr.h*k}" fill="${ink}" opacity=".8"/>`; continue; }
      out+=`<line x1="${x1}" y1="${y1}" x2="${x2}" y2="${y2}" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="10"/>`;
      if(o.type==='window') out+=`<line x1="${x1}" y1="${y1}" x2="${x2}" y2="${y2}" stroke="${ink2}" stroke-width="2"/>`;
      if(o.type==='door' && (o.to==='hall'||o.to==='street'||o.to==='corr')){ const mx=(x1+x2)/2, my=(y1+y2)/2, nx=-sg.nx, ny=-sg.ny, L=110;
        const sx=mx+nx*L, sy=my+ny*L, ex=mx+nx*14, ey=my+ny*14, lab=o.to==='hall'?'Трапезная':o.to==='street'?'Улица':'Коридор';
        const ah=12, px=-ny, py=nx;
        arrows+=`<line x1="${sx}" y1="${sy}" x2="${ex}" y2="${ey}" stroke="var(--accent)" stroke-width="3"/><polygon points="${ex},${ey} ${ex+nx*ah*1.6+px*ah},${ey+ny*ah*1.6+py*ah} ${ex+nx*ah*1.6-px*ah},${ey+ny*ah*1.6-py*ah}" fill="var(--accent)"/>`;
        const tx=sx+nx*22, ty=sy+ny*22, vert=nx!==0;
        arrows+=`<text x="${tx}" y="${ty}" font-size="18" font-weight="700" fill="var(--accent)" text-anchor="${vert?(nx>0?'start':'end'):'middle'}" dominant-baseline="central">${lab}</text>`; }
    }
    const ir=hasPoly(r)?innerRect(r):null, cx=X(r.x+(ir?ir.x+ir.w/2:r.w/2)), cy=Y(r.y+(ir?ir.y+ir.h/2:r.d/2)), fs=Math.max(14,Math.min(28,(ir?ir.w:r.w)*k/(r.name.length*0.62+1)));
    out+=`<text x="${cx}" y="${cy-fs*0.5}" font-size="${fs}" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle" fill="${ink}">${esc(r.name)}</text>
      <text x="${cx}" y="${cy+fs*0.6}" font-size="15" font-family="var(--f-mono)" text-anchor="middle" fill="${ink2}">${ir?`габарит ${fmt0(r.d*1000)} × ${fmt0(r.w*1000)} мм · ${fmt1(roomArea(r))} м²`:`${fmt0(r.d*1000)} × ${fmt0(r.w*1000)} мм · ${fmt1(roomArea(r))} м²`}</text>
      <text x="${cx}" y="${cy+fs*0.6+20}" font-size="13" text-anchor="middle" fill="var(--steel)">${esc(r.roles.map(x=>(ROLES.find(q=>q[0]===x)||[])[1]).filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'назначение не отмечено')}</text>`;
  }
  const w=(maxX-minX)*k+2*pad, h=(maxY-minY)*k+2*pad+40, kk=pickScale(w,h);
  const svg=`<svg viewBox="${-pad} ${-pad} ${w} ${h}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${w}" height="${h}"><rect x="${-pad}" y="${-pad}" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="#ffffff"/>${out}${arrows}${scaleBarSvg(0,(maxY-minY)*k+pad-10,ink,kk)}</svg>`;
  return {svg:finalizePrintSvg(svg,pal), wCm:w, hCm:h, scale:kk};
}
function swatchSvg(o){ // маленький образец для легенды, 28×14
  const pal=o.mono?PRINT_MONO:PRINT_PALETTE, ink=pal['--ink'];
  let inner='';
  if(o.zone){ inner = o.mono ? `${hatchDefs(true,ink)}<rect x="1" y="1" width="26" height="12" fill="url(#pz-${o.zone})" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="1"/>`
      : `<rect x="1" y="1" width="26" height="12" fill="${pal['--z-'+o.zone]}" fill-opacity=".25" stroke="${pal['--z-'+o.zone]}" stroke-width="1.5"/>`; }
  else if(o.kind==='have') inner=`<rect x="1" y="1" width="26" height="12" fill="none" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-dasharray="4 2"/>`;

  else if(o.kind==='hood') inner=`<rect x="1" y="1" width="26" height="12" fill="none" stroke="${o.mono?ink:pal['--z-hot']}" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-dasharray="5 3"/>`;
  else if(o.kind==='work') inner=`${hatchDefs(false,ink)}<rect x="1" y="1" width="26" height="12" fill="url(#pwz)" stroke="${pal['--ink-2']}" stroke-width="0.8" stroke-dasharray="2 2"/>`;
    const st=bad.has(u.key)?'var(--bad)':selSet.has(u.key)?'var(--ink)':warn.has(u.key)?'var(--warn)':c;
    const dash = wall ? 'stroke-dasharray="3 5"' : u.src==='have' ? 'stroke-dasharray="7 4"' : '';
    return `<g class="it" data-key="${esc(u.key)}" transform="translate(${b.x*100},${b.y*100})" ${mode!=='equip'?'style="pointer-events:none;opacity:.4"':''}>
      <rect width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="2" fill="${c}" fill-opacity="${wall?.06:u.src==='have'?.12:.24}" stroke="${st}" stroke-width="${selSet.has(u.key)||bad.has(u.key)?4:2}" ${dash}/>
      ${wall?'':`<line x1="${fe[0]}" y1="${fe[1]}" x2="${fe[2]}" y2="${fe[3]}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="6"/>`}
      <title>${esc(u.c.n)}${u.src==='have'?' (имеется)':''}</title>${eqLabel(u.c,w,h,u)}</g>`;
  }).join('');
  const walls=hasPoly(S.room)?wallsPathSvg(S.room,T,'var(--ink)'):`<g stroke="var(--ink)" stroke-width="${T}" stroke-linecap="square"><line x1="${-T/2}" y1="${-T/2}" x2="${W+T/2}" y2="${-T/2}"/><line x1="${W+T/2}" y1="${-T/2}" x2="${W+T/2}" y2="${D+T/2}"/><line x1="${-T/2}" y1="${D+T/2}" x2="${W+T/2}" y2="${D+T/2}"/><line x1="${-T/2}" y1="${-T/2}" x2="${-T/2}" y2="${D+T/2}"/></g>`;
  let swSvg='';
  if(mode==='equip' && !bare){ const g=curGeom(), all=layerOn('pts'), mx=swMax(g), list=[];
    for(const u of curUnits()){ if(!S.layout[u.key]||!hasDrain(u.c)) continue; const isS=sel===u.key&&selSet.size<2; if(!all&&!isS) continue;
      const r=sewerRoute(g,u,S.layout); if(r) list.push({r, emph:isS, label:isS||((r.blocked||r.len>mx+1e-6)&&scrK>=0.5)}); }   // на мелком плане (телефон) — подпись только у выбранного
    list.sort((a,b)=>a.emph-b.emph); swSvg=swRoutesSvg(g,list,scrK); }
  const ops=curGeom().openings.map(o=>openingSvg(o,o.derived&&mode==='room'?'view':mode)).join('');
  const showPts=!(mode==='room'&&bare) && (mode==='points' || layerOn('pts'));
  const pts=showPts ? S.points.filter(p=>p.type==='hood').map(p=>pointSvg(p,mode)).join('') + S.points.filter(p=>p.type!=='hood').map(p=>pointSvg(p,mode)).join('') : '';
